```
//! Группировка окон по приложению (`exe_path` или `class`).
//! nsbridge отдаёт клиентам готовые группы — виджеты не группируют.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::window::WindowInfo;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AppGroup {
    /// Стабильный ключ: `exe_path`, `class:...`, `id:...`.
    pub key: String,
    /// Одна буква для иконки.
    pub label: String,
    /// Все окна группы.
    pub windows: Vec<WindowInfo>,
    /// Активно хотя бы одно окно группы.
    pub has_active: bool,
    /// Все окна группы свёрнуты.
    pub all_hidden: bool,
}

pub fn group_windows(windows: Vec<WindowInfo>) -> Vec<AppGroup> {
    let mut map: HashMap<String, AppGroup> = HashMap::new();

    for win in windows {
        let key = if !win.exe_path.is_empty() {
            win.exe_path.clone()
        } else if !win.class.is_empty() {
            format!("class:{}", win.class)
        } else {
            format!("id:{}", win.id)
        };

        let group = map.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(|| {
            let label = if !win.class.is_empty() {
                win.class.chars().next().unwrap_or('?').to_uppercase().to_string()
            } else if !win.title.is_empty() {
                win.title.chars().next().unwrap_or('?').to_uppercase().to_string()
            } else {
                "?".to_string()
            };
            AppGroup {
                key,
                label,
                windows: Vec::new(),
                has_active: false,
                all_hidden: true,
            }
        });

        if win.is_active {
            group.has_active = true;
        }
        if !win.is_hidden {
            group.all_hidden = false;
        }
        group.windows.push(win);
    }

    let mut groups: Vec<AppGroup> = map.into_values().collect();
    groups.sort_by(|a, b| a.key.cmp(&b.key));
    groups
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/exe.rs ===
```
//! Извлечение иконок из Windows `.exe` (PE-файлов) и декодирование `.ico`.

use std::io::Cursor;
use std::path::Path;

use icoextract_rs::{IconExtractor, IconSelector};

pub struct DecodedIcon {
    pub rgba: Vec<u8>,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
}

pub fn extract_from_exe(exe_path: &Path) -> Option<DecodedIcon> {
    let extractor = IconExtractor::from_path(exe_path).ok()?;
    let icon = extractor.get_icon(IconSelector::Index(0)).ok()?;
    let ico_bytes = icon.to_ico_bytes().ok()?;
    decode_ico_bytes(&ico_bytes)
}

pub fn decode_ico_file(path: &Path) -> Option<DecodedIcon> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(path).ok()?;
    decode_ico_bytes(&bytes)
}

fn decode_ico_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<DecodedIcon> {
    use ico::IconDir;

    let dir = IconDir::read(Cursor::new(bytes)).ok()?;

    let mut best: Option<&ico::IconDirEntry> = None;
    let mut best_size = 0u32;
    for entry in dir.entries() {
        let size = (entry.width() as u32) * (entry.height() as u32);
        if size > best_size {
            best_size = size;
            best = Some(entry);
        }
    }

    let entry = best?;
    let image = entry.decode().ok()?;

    let (w, h) = (image.width() as usize, image.height() as usize);
    let rgba = image.rgba_data().to_vec();

    Some(DecodedIcon {
        rgba,
        width: w,
        height: h,
    })
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/mod.rs ===
```
//! Поиск и кеширование иконок для окон и трея.

pub mod store;
mod exe;
mod png;
mod resize;
mod resolver;
mod svg;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

/// Размер, в котором nsbridge хранит все иконки в кеше.
pub const ICON_CACHE_SIZE: u32 = 64;

/// Публичная обёртка над `resize::resize` (RGBA → RGBA) для `tray.rs`.
pub fn resize_public(
    rgba: &[u8],
    src_w: usize,
    src_h: usize,
    dst_w: usize,
    dst_h: usize,
) -> Vec<u8> {
    resize::resize(rgba, src_w, src_h, dst_w, dst_h)
}

pub fn resolve_icon_path(exe_path: &str, class: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let key = if !exe_path.is_empty() {
        exe_path
    } else {
        class
    };
    if key.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }

    if let Some(p) = store::icon_path(key) {
        return Some(p);
    }

    // Wine/PE — извлекаем иконку из .exe (как раньше).
    if exe_path.to_lowercase().ends_with(".exe") {
        if let Some(i) = exe::extract_from_exe(Path::new(exe_path)) {
            return normalize_and_save(key, &i.rgba, i.width, i.height);
        }
        return None;
    }

    // Стандартный путь: resolver (desktop / class / exe stem) -> файл.
    if let Some(path) = resolver::resolve_icon_path(exe_path, class) {
        if let Some((rgba, w, h)) = decode_by_ext(&path) {
            return normalize_and_save(key, &rgba, w, h);
        }
    }

    None
}

pub fn resolve_icon_name(name: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if name.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }

    let candidates: Vec<&str> = if let Some(base) = name.strip_suffix("-symbolic") {
        if base.is_empty() {
            vec![name]
        } else {
            vec![base, name]
        }
    } else {
        vec![name]
    };

    for cand in candidates {
        let key = format!("icon:{cand}");
        if let Some(p) = store::icon_path(&key) {
            return Some(p);
        }
        if let Some(path) = resolver::find_icon_path(cand) {
            if let Some((rgba, w, h)) = decode_by_ext(&path) {
                if let Some(saved) = normalize_and_save(&key, &rgba, w, h) {
                    return Some(saved);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    None
}

fn decode_by_ext(path: &Path) -> Option<(Vec<u8>, usize, usize)> {
    let ext = path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
    if ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("svg") {
        svg::decode(path, ICON_CACHE_SIZE).map(|i| (i.rgba, i.width, i.height))
    } else if ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ico") {
        exe::decode_ico_file(path).map(|i| (i.rgba, i.width, i.height))
    } else {
        png::decode(path).map(|i| (i.rgba, i.width, i.height))
    }
}

fn normalize_and_save(key: &str, rgba: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let (nw, nh) = (ICON_CACHE_SIZE as usize, ICON_CACHE_SIZE as usize);
    let normalized: Vec<u8> = if w == nw && h == nh {
        rgba.to_vec()
    } else {
        resize::resize(rgba, w, h, nw, nh)
    };
    store::save_icon_rgba(key, ICON_CACHE_SIZE, ICON_CACHE_SIZE, &normalized)
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/png.rs ===
```
//! Обертка над крейтом `png` для чтения изображений.
//! Приводит любой PNG (RGB, RGBA, Grayscale, Palette) к сырому RGBA8 буферу.
//!
//! Для `Indexed` читаем `tRNS` (прозрачность палитры).

use std::io::BufReader;
use std::path::Path;

/// Результат декодирования PNG: (RGBA-байты, ширина, высота).
pub struct DecodedPng {
    pub rgba: Vec<u8>,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
}

/// Прочитать PNG-файл и вернуть RGBA-буфер.
pub fn decode(path: &Path) -> Option<DecodedPng> {
    let file = std::fs::File::open(path).ok()?;
    let reader = BufReader::new(file);
    let decoder = png::Decoder::new(reader);

    let mut reader = decoder.read_info().ok()?;
    let mut buf = vec![0; reader.output_buffer_size()?];
    let info = reader.next_frame(&mut buf).ok()?;
    let raw = &buf[..info.buffer_size()];

    let (w, h) = (info.width as usize, info.height as usize);

    let rgba = match info.color_type {
        png::ColorType::Rgba => raw.to_vec(),

        png::ColorType::Rgb => {
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(w * h * 4);
            for chunk in raw.chunks(3) {
                out.extend_from_slice(chunk);
                out.push(255);
            }
            out
        }

        png::ColorType::Grayscale => {
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(w * h * 4);
            for &g in raw {
                out.extend_from_slice(&[g, g, g, 255]);
            }
            out
        }

        png::ColorType::GrayscaleAlpha => {
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(w * h * 4);
            for chunk in raw.chunks(2) {
                let (g, a) = (chunk[0], chunk[1]);
                out.extend_from_slice(&[g, g, g, a]);
            }
            out
        }

        png::ColorType::Indexed => {
            let palette = reader.info().palette.as_ref()?;
            let trns = reader.info().trns.clone();

            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(w * h * 4);
            for &idx in raw {
                let i = (idx as usize) * 3;
                if i + 2 < palette.len() {
                    let r = palette[i];
                    let g = palette[i + 1];
                    let b = palette[i + 2];
                    let a = match &trns {
                        Some(t) => *t.get(idx as usize).unwrap_or(&255),
                        None => 255,
                    };
                    out.extend_from_slice(&[r, g, b, a]);
                } else {
                    out.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 255]);
                }
            }
            out
        }
    };

    Some(DecodedPng {
        rgba,
        width: w,
        height: h,
    })
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/resize.rs ===
```
//! Билинейный ресайз RGBA → RGBA. Достаточно для иконок.

pub fn resize(
    rgba: &[u8],
    src_w: usize,
    src_h: usize,
    dst_w: usize,
    dst_h: usize,
) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(dst_w * dst_h * 4);
    let scale_x = src_w as f32 / dst_w as f32;
    let scale_y = src_h as f32 / dst_h as f32;

    for dy in 0..dst_h {
        for dx in 0..dst_w {
            let sx = (dx as f32 + 0.5) * scale_x - 0.5;
            let sy = (dy as f32 + 0.5) * scale_y - 0.5;

            let x0 = sx.floor() as isize;
            let y0 = sy.floor() as isize;
            let x1 = x0 + 1;
            let y1 = y0 + 1;

            let fx = sx - x0 as f32;
            let fy = sy - y0 as f32;

            let c00 = get_pixel(rgba, src_w, src_h, x0, y0);
            let c10 = get_pixel(rgba, src_w, src_h, x1, y0);
            let c01 = get_pixel(rgba, src_w, src_h, x0, y1);
            let c11 = get_pixel(rgba, src_w, src_h, x1, y1);

            let mut c = [0u8; 4];
            for k in 0..4 {
                let top = c00[k] as f32 * (1.0 - fx) + c10[k] as f32 * fx;
                let bot = c01[k] as f32 * (1.0 - fx) + c11[k] as f32 * fx;
                c[k] = (top * (1.0 - fy) + bot * fy).round().clamp(0.0, 255.0) as u8;
            }
            out.extend_from_slice(&c);
        }
    }
    out
}

fn get_pixel(rgba: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, x: isize, y: isize) -> [u8; 4] {
    let x = x.max(0).min(w as isize - 1) as usize;
    let y = y.max(0).min(h as isize - 1) as usize;
    let i = (y * w + x) * 4;
    [rgba[i], rgba[i + 1], rgba[i + 2], rgba[i + 3]]
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/resolver.rs ===
```
//! Поиск иконок по стандарту Freedesktop.
//!
//! Имя иконки берём из `.desktop` (`Icon=`), если есть. Если нет —
//! пробуем `class` окна и basename `exe_path`. Сам файл иконки ищем
//! через `freedesktop-icons`, который читает `index.theme`, ходит по
//! `Inherits` и гарантированно доходит до `hicolor`.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use freedesktop_icons::lookup;

/// Найти имя иконки (`Icon=`) в `.desktop` по `exe_path` или `class`.
/// Пропускает `.desktop` с пустым `Icon=`.
fn find_icon_name(exe_path: &str, class: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let search_dirs = vec![
        PathBuf::from("/usr/share/applications"),
        dirs_home().join(".local/share/applications"),
        dirs_home().join(".local/share/applications/wine/Programs"),
    ];

    for dir in search_dirs {
        if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let path = entry.path();
                if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) != Some("desktop") {
                    continue;
                }
                if let Some((icon, exec)) = parse_desktop(&path) {
                    if icon.is_empty() {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if !exec.is_empty() && !exe_path.is_empty() {
                        if exec == exe_path || exe_path.contains(&exec) {
                            return Some(icon);
                        }
                    }
                    if !class.is_empty() && icon.to_lowercase() == class.to_lowercase() {
                        return Some(icon);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    None
}

fn parse_desktop(path: &Path) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;
    let mut icon = String::new();
    let mut exec = String::new();

    for line in content.lines() {
        let line = line.trim();
        if let Some(rest) = line.strip_prefix("Icon=") {
            icon = rest.to_string();
        } else if let Some(rest) = line.strip_prefix("Exec=") {
            exec = rest.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
        }
    }

    if icon.is_empty() && exec.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some((icon, exec))
    }
}

/// Найти файл иконки по имени через `freedesktop-icons`.
/// Размер 64 — под `ICON_CACHE_SIZE`. Кэш включён: повторные вызовы
/// для того же имени не будут заново обходить директории.
pub fn find_icon_path(icon_name: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if icon_name.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    lookup(icon_name)
        .with_size(64)
        .with_cache()
        .find()
}

fn dirs_home() -> PathBuf {
    std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"))
}

/// Главная точка входа: `exe_path` + `class` → путь к файлу иконки.
/// Порядок кандидатов:
/// 1. `Icon=` из `.desktop` (если найден и непустой).
/// 2. `class` как имя иконки.
/// 3. basename `exe_path` (без расширения).
pub fn resolve_icon_path(exe_path: &str, class: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    // 1. .desktop -> Icon= -> lookup.
    if let Some(icon_name) = find_icon_name(exe_path, class) {
        if let Some(p) = find_icon_path(&icon_name) {
            return Some(p);
        }
    }

    // 2. class напрямую (mpv -> mpv).
    if !class.is_empty() {
        if let Some(p) = find_icon_path(class) {
            return Some(p);
        }
    }

    // 3. basename exe (mpv для /usr/bin/mpv).
    if !exe_path.is_empty() {
        if let Some(base) = Path::new(exe_path).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {
            if base.to_lowercase() != class.to_lowercase() {
                if let Some(p) = find_icon_path(base) {
                    return Some(p);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    None
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/store.rs ===
```
//! Хранилище иконок на диске (кеш nsbridge).
//!
//! Структура:
//! ~/.cache/nsbridge/icons/<key>/
//!   └── icon.png   (нормализованный, 64×64)

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

/// Базовая папка для хранения иконок.
pub fn base_dir() -> PathBuf {
    if let Some(xdg) = std::env::var_os("XDG_CACHE_HOME") {
        if !xdg.is_empty() {
            return PathBuf::from(xdg).join("nsbridge/icons");
        }
    }
    let home = std::env::var_os("HOME")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
    home.join(".cache/nsbridge/icons")
}

/// Папка для конкретного приложения (по ключу группы).
pub fn app_dir(key: &str) -> PathBuf {
    base_dir().join(sanitize_key(key))
}

/// Очистить ключ от символов, недопустимых в имени папки.
fn sanitize_key(key: &str) -> String {
    key.chars()
        .map(|c| if c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '-' || c == '_' || c == '.' { c } else { '_' })
        .collect()
}

/// Сохранить RGBA-байты как PNG в папку приложения.
pub fn save_icon_rgba(key: &str, w: u32, h: u32, rgba: &[u8]) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let dir = app_dir(key);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).ok()?;
    let path = dir.join("icon.png");

    let file = std::fs::File::create(&path).ok()?;
    let w_buf = std::io::BufWriter::new(file);
    let mut encoder = png::Encoder::new(w_buf, w, h);
    encoder.set_color(png::ColorType::Rgba);
    encoder.set_depth(png::BitDepth::Eight);
    let mut writer = encoder.write_header().ok()?;
    writer.write_image_data(rgba).ok()?;
    writer.finish().ok()?;

    Some(path)
}

/// Скопировать готовый PNG/ICO файл в кеш.
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn save_icon_file(key: &str, src: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let dir = app_dir(key);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).ok()?;
    let dst = dir.join("icon.png");

    let ext = src.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
    if ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("png") {
        std::fs::copy(src, &dst).ok()?;
        Some(dst)
    } else if ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ico") {
        if let Some(icon) = super::exe::decode_ico_file(src) {
            save_icon_rgba(key, icon.width as u32, icon.height as u32, &icon.rgba)
        } else {
            None
        }
    } else {
        None
    }
}

/// Проверить, есть ли готовая иконка для ключа.
pub fn has_icon(key: &str) -> bool {
    app_dir(key).join("icon.png").exists()
}

/// Вернуть путь к готовой иконке (если есть).
pub fn icon_path(key: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let p = app_dir(key).join("icon.png");
    if p.exists() { Some(p) } else { None }
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/icons/svg.rs ===
```
//! Декодирование SVG в RGBA-буфер с помощью usvg + tiny-skia.

use std::path::Path;

use tiny_skia::Pixmap;
use usvg::{Options, Tree};

pub struct DecodedSvg {
    pub rgba: Vec<u8>,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
}

pub fn decode(path: &Path, size: u32) -> Option<DecodedSvg> {
    let svg_data = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;

    let opt = Options::default();
    let tree = Tree::from_str(&svg_data, &opt).ok()?;

    let svg_size = tree.size();
    let svg_w = svg_size.width();
    let svg_h = svg_size.height();

    let scale = (size as f32 / svg_w.max(svg_h)).min(1.0);
    let out_w = (svg_w * scale).ceil().max(1.0) as u32;
    let out_h = (svg_h * scale).ceil().max(1.0) as u32;

    let mut pixmap = Pixmap::new(out_w, out_h)?;
    resvg::render(
        &tree,
        tiny_skia::Transform::from_scale(scale, scale),
        &mut pixmap.as_mut(),
    );

    let mut rgba = Vec::with_capacity(pixmap.data().len());
    for chunk in pixmap.data().chunks(4) {
        let (r, g, b, a) = (chunk[0], chunk[1], chunk[2], chunk[3]);
        if a > 0 {
            let inv_a = 255.0 / a as f32;
            rgba.push((r as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8);
            rgba.push((g as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8);
            rgba.push((b as f32 * inv_a).min(255.0) as u8);
            rgba.push(a);
        } else {
            rgba.push(0);
            rgba.push(0);
            rgba.push(0);
            rgba.push(0);
        }
    }

    Some(DecodedSvg {
        rgba,
        width: out_w as usize,
        height: out_h as usize,
    })
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/ipc.rs ===
```
//! Unix-сокет-сервер для nsbridge.
//!
//! Клиенты (панель, виджеты) подключаются, читают группы, шлют команды.
//! nsbridge рассылает всем клиентам новые снимки при изменениях.
//!
//! ВАЖНО: мёртвые клиенты (EOF при чтении или ошибка при записи) сразу
//! удаляются из списка. Иначе при частых переподключениях панели они
//! накапливаются, broadcast пишет в них, получает BrokenPipe, а новые
//! клиенты (только что подключившиеся) могут быть ошибочно удалены
//! при очистке "мёртвых" индексов.

use std::io::{self, Read};
use std::os::fd::{AsRawFd, RawFd};
use std::os::unix::net::{UnixListener, UnixStream};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use crate::protocol;

pub fn socket_path() -> PathBuf {
    let uid = nix::unistd::getuid();
    PathBuf::from(format!("/run/user/{}/nsbridge.sock", uid))
}

struct Client {
    stream: UnixStream,
    buffer: Vec<u8>,
}

pub struct IpcServer {
    listener: UnixListener,
    path: PathBuf,
    clients: Vec<Client>,
}

impl IpcServer {
    pub fn bind<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let path = path.as_ref().to_path_buf();
        if path.exists() {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        }
        if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(dir);
        }
        let listener = UnixListener::bind(&path)?;
        listener.set_nonblocking(true)?;
        Ok(Self {
            listener,
            path,
            clients: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn accept_pending(&mut self) {
        loop {
            match self.listener.accept() {
                Ok((stream, _)) => {
                    let _ = stream.set_nonblocking(true);
                    eprintln!("[nsbridge] client connected (total {})", self.clients.len() + 1);
                    self.clients.push(Client {
                        stream,
                        buffer: Vec::new(),
                    });
                }
                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => break,
                Err(e) => {
                    eprintln!("[nsbridge] accept error: {e}");
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn broadcast(&mut self, msg: &str) {
        let mut dead = Vec::new();
        for (i, client) in self.clients.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if let Err(e) = protocol::write_frame(&mut client.stream, msg) {
                match e.kind() {
                    io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe
                    | io::ErrorKind::ConnectionReset
                    | io::ErrorKind::WriteZero => {
                        dead.push(i);
                    }
                    _ => {
                        eprintln!("[nsbridge] write error to client {i}: {e}");
                        dead.push(i);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for i in dead.into_iter().rev() {
            let _ = self.clients.remove(i);
            eprintln!("[nsbridge] client disconnected (after broadcast), total {}", self.clients.len());
        }
    }

    pub fn clients_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.clients.len()
    }

    /// fd всех клиентов (для `poll` в `main.rs`).
    /// Порядок соответствует `clients` (индекс 0..N).
    pub fn client_fds(&self) -> Vec<RawFd> {
        self.clients
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.stream.as_raw_fd())
            .collect()
    }

    /// Прочитать команды от клиентов. Возвращает список `(index, payload)`.
    /// Если клиент отключился (EOF), он удаляется из списка.
    pub fn read_client_messages(&mut self) -> Vec<(usize, String)> {
        let mut messages = Vec::new();
        let mut dead = Vec::new();

        for (i, client) in self.clients.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            let mut temp = [0u8; 4096];
            let mut is_eof = false;
            loop {
                match client.stream.read(&mut temp) {
                    Ok(0) => {
                        is_eof = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    Ok(n) => client.buffer.extend_from_slice(&temp[..n]),
                    Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => break,
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("[nsbridge] read error client {i}: {e}");
                        is_eof = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if is_eof {
                dead.push(i);
                continue;
            }

            let mut offset = 0;
            while client.buffer.len() - offset >= 4 {
                let len = u32::from_be_bytes([
                    client.buffer[offset],
                    client.buffer[offset + 1],
                    client.buffer[offset + 2],
                    client.buffer[offset + 3],
                ]) as usize;
                if client.buffer.len() - offset - 4 < len {
                    break;
                }
                let payload = client.buffer[offset + 4..offset + 4 + len].to_vec();
                messages.push((i, String::from_utf8_lossy(&payload).to_string()));
                offset += 4 + len;
            }
            if offset > 0 {
                client.buffer.drain(..offset);
            }
        }

        for i in dead.into_iter().rev() {
            let _ = self.clients.remove(i);
            eprintln!("[nsbridge] client disconnected (after read), total {}", self.clients.len());
        }

        messages
    }

    pub fn send_to(&mut self, index: usize, msg: &str) {
        if let Some(client) = self.clients.get_mut(index) {
            if let Err(e) = protocol::write_frame(&mut client.stream, msg) {
                eprintln!("[nsbridge] send_to client {index} error: {e}, removing");
                self.clients.remove(index);
                eprintln!("[nsbridge] client disconnected (after send_to), total {}", self.clients.len());
            }
        }
    }
}

impl AsRawFd for IpcServer {
    fn as_raw_fd(&self) -> RawFd {
        self.listener.as_raw_fd()
    }
}

impl Drop for IpcServer {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.path);
    }
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/main.rs ===
```
mod dbus;
mod group;
mod icons;
mod ipc;
mod proc;
mod protocol;
mod tray;
mod window;
mod x11;

use std::os::fd::{AsRawFd, BorrowedFd, RawFd};
use std::path::PathBuf;

use nix::poll::{poll, PollFd, PollFlags, PollTimeout};
use x11rb::connection::Connection;

use crate::dbus::DBus;
use crate::group::group_windows;
use crate::ipc::{socket_path, IpcServer};
use crate::protocol::{encode_groups, encode_tray, ClientMsg};
use crate::tray::TrayState;
use crate::window::WindowInfo;
use crate::x11::X11;

fn main() {
    if let Err(e) = run() {
        eprintln!("nsbridge: fatal error: {e}");
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
}

fn broadcast_all(
    server: &mut IpcServer,
    prev_windows: &[WindowInfo],
    tray: &mut TrayState,
) {
    let groups = group_windows(prev_windows.to_vec());
    let msg_groups = encode_groups(&groups);
    let tray_items = tray.snapshot();
    let msg_tray = encode_tray(&tray_items);
    server.broadcast(&msg_groups);
    server.broadcast(&msg_tray);
}

fn send_all(
    server: &mut IpcServer,
    idx: usize,
    prev_windows: &[WindowInfo],
    tray: &mut TrayState,
) {
    let groups = group_windows(prev_windows.to_vec());
    let msg_groups = encode_groups(&groups);
    server.send_to(idx, &msg_groups);

    let tray_items = tray.snapshot();
    let msg_tray = encode_tray(&tray_items);
    server.send_to(idx, &msg_tray);
}

/// Восстановить иконки для закреплённых приложений, даже если они
/// не запущены. `pinned.json` хранит `key` (exe_path или `class:...`)
/// и абсолютный путь к `icon.png` в кэше nsbridge. Если кэш снесён —
/// путь битый. Здесь по `key` заново резолвим иконку через ту же
/// логику (`icons::resolve_icon_path`), что и для живых окон, и
/// записываем `icon.png` в тот же каталог (`store::app_dir(key)`),
/// поэтому путь в `pinned.json` снова становится валидным.
fn restore_pinned_icons() {
    let home = match std::env::var_os("HOME") {
        Some(h) => PathBuf::from(h),
        None => return,
    };
    let path = home.join(".config/nspanel/widgets/window_list_rust/pinned.json");
    let content = match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
        Ok(c) => c,
        Err(_) => return,
    };
    let arr: Vec<serde_json::Value> = match serde_json::from_str(&content) {
        Ok(a) => a,
        Err(_) => {
            eprintln!("[nsbridge] pinned.json parse failed, skipping restore");
            return;
        }
    };

    let mut restored = 0usize;
    let mut skipped = 0usize;

    for item in arr {
        let key = match item.get("key").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
            Some(k) if !k.is_empty() => k,
            _ => continue,
        };

        // Уже есть в кэше — не трогаем.
        if icons::store::icon_path(key).is_some() {
            skipped += 1;
            continue;
        }

        let (exe, class) = if let Some(c) = key.strip_prefix("class:") {
            ("", c)
        } else if key.starts_with("id:") {
            // Нестабильный ключ (id окна) — восстановить нечего.
            continue;
        } else {
            (key, "")
        };

        if icons::resolve_icon_path(exe, class).is_some() {
            restored += 1;
            eprintln!("[nsbridge] restored pinned icon: {key}");
        } else {
            eprintln!("[nsbridge] pinned icon not found: {key}");
        }
    }

    if restored > 0 || skipped > 0 {
        eprintln!(
            "[nsbridge] pinned icons: restored {}, already cached {}",
            restored, skipped
        );
    }
}

fn run() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    eprintln!("[nsbridge] starting");

    let x11 = X11::connect()?;
    eprintln!("[nsbridge] connected to X11, root=0x{:x}", x11.root);
    let _ = x11.subscribe_all_windows();

    // Восстанавливаем иконки закреплённых до того, как панель подключится
    // и начнёт запрашивать пути.
    restore_pinned_icons();

    let mut server = IpcServer::bind(socket_path())?;
    eprintln!("[nsbridge] listening on {}", socket_path().display());

    let mut dbus_conn: Option<DBus> = match DBus::session() {
        Ok(d) => Some(d),
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("[dbus] session bus connect failed: {e} (tray disabled)");
            None
        }
    };
    let mut tray = TrayState::new();
    if let Some(dbus) = dbus_conn.as_mut() {
        match tray.subscribe(dbus) {
            Ok(()) => eprintln!("[dbus][tray] ready, {} items", tray.items().len()),
            Err(e) => eprintln!("[dbus][tray] subscribe failed: {e} (tray may be empty)"),
        }
    }

    let mut prev_windows: Vec<WindowInfo> = Vec::new();
    let mut has_prev = false;

    let x11_fd = x11.conn.stream().as_raw_fd();

    loop {
        let mut raw_fds: Vec<RawFd> = Vec::new();
        raw_fds.push(x11_fd);
        raw_fds.push(server.as_raw_fd());
        let client_fds = server.client_fds();
        raw_fds.extend(client_fds.iter().copied());

        let mut fds: Vec<PollFd> = Vec::new();
        for fd in &raw_fds {
            fds.push(PollFd::new(
                unsafe { BorrowedFd::borrow_raw(*fd) },
                PollFlags::POLLIN,
            ));
        }

        match poll(&mut fds, PollTimeout::NONE) {
            Ok(0) => continue,
            Ok(_) => {}
            Err(nix::errno::Errno::EINTR) => continue,
            Err(e) => return Err(format!("poll failed: {e}").into()),
        }

        // --- accept ---
        if let Some(revents) = fds[1].revents() {
            if revents.contains(PollFlags::POLLIN) {
                server.accept_pending();

                if !has_prev {
                    prev_windows = x11.collect_windows();
                    has_prev = true;
                }
                broadcast_all(&mut server, &prev_windows, &mut tray);
            }
        }

        // --- X11 ---
        if let Some(revents) = fds[0].revents() {
            if revents.contains(PollFlags::POLLIN) {
                loop {
                    let ev = match x11.conn.poll_for_event() {
                        Ok(Some(ev)) => ev,
                        Ok(None) => break,
                        Err(e) => return Err(format!("x11 poll_for_event: {e}").into()),
                    };

                    server.accept_pending();

                    match ev {
                        x11rb::protocol::Event::PropertyNotify(e) => {
                            let relevant = if e.window == x11.root {
                                e.atom == x11.atoms.net_client_list
                                    || e.atom == x11.atoms.net_active_window
                            } else {
                                e.atom == x11.atoms.net_wm_state
                            };
                            if !relevant {
                                continue;
                            }

                            if e.window == x11.root
                                && e.atom == x11.atoms.net_client_list
                            {
                                let _ = x11.subscribe_all_windows();
                            }

                            let wins = x11.collect_windows();
                            let changed = !has_prev || windows_changed(&prev_windows, &wins);
                            if has_prev && !changed {
                                continue;
                            }
                            prev_windows = wins;
                            has_prev = true;

                            if server.clients_count() > 0 {
                                broadcast_all(&mut server, &prev_windows, &mut tray);
                            }
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // --- D-Bus ---
        if let Some(dbus) = dbus_conn.as_mut() {
            let msgs = dbus.poll_messages();
            let mut tray_changed = false;
            for msg in &msgs {
                if tray.handle_message(dbus, msg) {
                    tray_changed = true;
                }
            }
            if tray_changed && server.clients_count() > 0 {
                let tray_items = tray.snapshot();
                let msg = encode_tray(&tray_items);
                server.broadcast(&msg);
            }
        }

        // --- clients ---
        let mut client_has_data = false;
        let client_start = 2;
        let client_end = client_start + server.client_fds().len();
        for i in client_start..client_end {
            if i >= fds.len() {
                break;
            }
            if let Some(revents) = fds[i].revents() {
                if revents.contains(PollFlags::POLLIN) {
                    client_has_data = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if client_has_data {
            let messages = server.read_client_messages();
            for (idx, payload) in messages {
                match serde_json::from_str::<ClientMsg>(&payload) {
                    Ok(ClientMsg::Activate { id }) => {
                        let _ = x11.activate_window(id);
                    }
                    Ok(ClientMsg::Toggle { id }) => {
                        let _ = x11.toggle_window(id);
                    }
                    Ok(ClientMsg::Request) => {
                        if !has_prev {
                            prev_windows = x11.collect_windows();
                            has_prev = true;
                        }
                        send_all(&mut server, idx, &prev_windows, &mut tray);
                    }
                    Ok(ClientMsg::TrayClick { dest, path, button, x, y }) => {
                        if let Some(dbus) = dbus_conn.as_mut() {
                            if let Err(e) = tray.activate(dbus, &dest, &path, button, x, y) {
                                eprintln!("[dbus][tray] activate failed: {e}");
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("[nsbridge] bad client message: {e} ({payload})");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn windows_changed(a: &[WindowInfo], b: &[WindowInfo]) -> bool {
    if a.len() != b.len() {
        return true;
    }
    for (x, y) in a.iter().zip(b.iter()) {
        if x.id != y.id
            || x.is_active != y.is_active
            || x.is_hidden != y.is_hidden
            || x.title != y.title
            || x.class != y.class
            || x.exe_path != y.exe_path
            || x.icon_path != y.icon_path
        {
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/proc.rs ===
```
//! /proc: путь к бинарнику и тип приложения.

use std::fs;
use std::io::Read;

use crate::window::BinaryKind;

pub fn resolve_exe(pid: u32) -> (String, BinaryKind) {
    let exe_link = format!("/proc/{pid}/exe");
    let exe_path = match fs::read_link(&exe_link) {
        Ok(p) => p.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
        Err(_) => return (String::new(), BinaryKind::Unknown),
    };

    let base = exe_path.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or("");

    if matches!(
        base,
        "wine" | "wine64" | "wine-preloader" | "wine64-preloader"
    ) {
        if let Some(w) = wine_exe_from_maps(pid) {
            return (w, BinaryKind::Wine);
        }
        return (exe_path, BinaryKind::Wine);
    }

    if base == "bwrap" {
        return (exe_path, BinaryKind::Flatpak);
    }

    (exe_path, BinaryKind::Native)
}

fn wine_exe_from_maps(pid: u32) -> Option<String> {
    let maps_path = format!("/proc/{pid}/maps");
    let mut f = fs::File::open(&maps_path).ok()?;
    let mut content = String::new();
    f.read_to_string(&mut content).ok()?;

    let mut best: Option<(u64, String)> = None;
    for line in content.lines() {
        let mut it = line.split_whitespace();
        let range = it.next()?;
        let _perms = it.next();
        let _offset = it.next();
        let _dev = it.next();
        let _inode = it.next();
        let path = match it.next() {
            Some(p) => p,
            None => continue,
        };
        if !path.to_lowercase().ends_with(".exe") {
            continue;
        }
        let mut parts = range.split('-');
        let a = parts.next().and_then(|s| u64::from_str_radix(s, 16).ok());
        let b = parts.next().and_then(|s| u64::from_str_radix(s, 16).ok());
        if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (a, b) {
            let size = b.saturating_sub(a);
            if best.as_ref().map(|(s, _)| size > *s).unwrap_or(true) {
                best = Some((size, path.to_string()));
            }
        }
    }
    best.map(|(_, p)| p)
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/protocol.rs ===
```
//! Протокол обмена сообщениями между `nsbridge` и клиентами (панелью).
//!
//! Клиент → nsbridge (JSON):
//!   {"type":"request"}
//!   {"type":"toggle","id":N}
//!   {"type":"activate","id":N}
//!   {"type":"tray_click","dest":":1.42","path":"/org/kind/StatusNotifierItem","button":1,"x":5,"y":5}
//!
//! nsbridge → клиент (JSON):
//!   {"type":"groups","data":[...]}
//!   {"type":"tray","data":[...]}

use std::io::{self, Read, Write};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::group::AppGroup;
use crate::tray::TrayOut;
use crate::window::{BinaryKind, WindowInfo};

/// Сообщение от клиента (панели).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ClientMsg {
    Request,
    Toggle { id: u32 },
    Activate { id: u32 },
    TrayClick {
        dest: String,
        path: String,
        button: u8,
        x: i32,
        y: i32,
    },
}

/// Сформировать JSON `groups`.
pub fn encode_groups(groups: &[AppGroup]) -> String {
    #[derive(Serialize)]
    struct Envelope<'a> {
        #[serde(rename = "type")]
        kind: &'a str,
        data: &'a [AppGroup],
    }
    let env = Envelope { kind: "groups", data: groups };
    serde_json::to_string(&env).unwrap_or_else(|_| r#"{"type":"groups","data":[]}"#.to_string())
}

/// Сформировать JSON `tray`.
pub fn encode_tray(items: &[TrayOut]) -> String {
    #[derive(Serialize)]
    struct Out<'a> {
        dest: &'a str,
        path: &'a str,
        title: &'a str,
        icon_path: &'a Option<String>,
        needs_attention: bool,
        status: &'a str,
    }
    let data: Vec<Out> = items
        .iter()
        .map(|it| Out {
            dest: &it.dest,
            path: &it.path,
            title: &it.title,
            icon_path: &it.icon_path,
            needs_attention: it.needs_attention,
            status: &it.status,
        })
        .collect();
    #[derive(Serialize)]
    struct Envelope<'a> {
        #[serde(rename = "type")]
        kind: &'a str,
        data: &'a [Out<'a>],
    }
    let env = Envelope { kind: "tray", data: &data };
    serde_json::to_string(&env).unwrap_or_else(|_| r#"{"type":"tray","data":[]}"#.to_string())
}

/// Записать сообщение в поток: 4 байта длины + текст.
pub fn write_frame<W: Write>(w: &mut W, msg: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    let len = msg.len() as u32;
    w.write_all(&len.to_be_bytes())?;
    w.write_all(msg.as_bytes())?;
    w.flush()
}

/// Прочитать сообщение из потока (для клиентов — панели).
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn read_frame<R: Read>(r: &mut R) -> io::Result<Option<String>> {
    let mut len_buf = [0u8; 4];
    match r.read_exact(&mut len_buf) {
        Ok(()) => {}
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::UnexpectedEof => return Ok(None),
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    }
    let len = u32::from_be_bytes(len_buf) as usize;
    if len > 16 * 1024 * 1024 {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "message too large",
        ));
    }
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; len];
    r.read_exact(&mut buf)?;
    Ok(Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf).to_string()))
}

/// Старый текстовый `encode_windows` (оставлен для отладки).
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn encode_windows(windows: &[WindowInfo]) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    out.push_str("type=windows\n");
    for w in windows {
        let icon = match &w.icon_path {
            Some(p) => p.clone(),
            None => "-".to_string(),
        };
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "win=0x{:x} pid={} class={} instance={} title={} exe={} kind={} active={} hidden={} icon={}\n",
            w.id,
            w.pid.map(|p| p.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "-".to_string()),
            esc(&w.class),
            esc(&w.instance),
            esc(&w.title),
            esc(&w.exe_path),
            w.kind.as_str(),
            if w.is_active { "1" } else { "0" },
            if w.is_hidden { "1" } else { "0" },
            esc(&icon),
        ));
    }
    out
}

fn esc(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    for ch in s.chars() {
        match ch {
            ' ' => out.push_str("\\s"),
            '=' => out.push_str("\\e"),
            '\\' => out.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => out.push_str("\\n"),
            _ => out.push(ch),
        }
    }
    out
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn unesc(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
    while let Some(ch) = chars.next() {
        if ch == '\\' {
            match chars.next() {
                Some('s') => out.push(' '),
                Some('e') => out.push('='),
                Some('\\') => out.push('\\'),
                Some('n') => out.push('\n'),
                Some(other) => {
                    out.push('\\');
                    out.push(other);
                }
                None => out.push('\\'),
            }
        } else {
            out.push(ch);
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Разобрать текстовое `windows` обратно в `Vec<WindowInfo>` (для отладки).
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn decode_windows(msg: &str) -> Vec<WindowInfo> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for line in msg.lines() {
        if !line.starts_with("win=") {
            continue;
        }
        let mut w = WindowInfo {
            id: 0,
            pid: None,
            title: String::new(),
            class: String::new(),
            instance: String::new(),
            exe_path: String::new(),
            kind: BinaryKind::Unknown,
            is_active: false,
            is_hidden: false,
            icon_path: None,
        };
        let mut rest = line;
        if let Some(space) = rest.find(' ') {
            let first = &rest[..space];
            rest = &rest[space + 1..];
            if let Some(id_hex) = first.strip_prefix("win=0x") {
                w.id = u32::from_str_radix(id_hex, 16).unwrap_or(0);
            }
        }
        let keys = ["pid", "class", "instance", "title", "exe", "kind", "active", "hidden", "icon"];
        let mut parts: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
        let mut current_key: Option<String> = None;
        let mut current_val = String::new();
        let mut chars = rest.chars().peekable();
        while let Some(ch) = chars.next() {
            if ch == ' ' {
                let mut peek = chars.clone();
                let mut word = String::new();
                while let Some(&c) = peek.peek() {
                    if c == '=' { break; }
                    if c == ' ' { break; }
                    word.push(c);
                    peek.next();
                }
                if peek.peek() == Some(&'=') && keys.contains(&word.as_str()) {
                    if let Some(k) = current_key.take() {
                        parts.push((k, current_val.clone()));
                    }
                    current_val.clear();
                    let wlen = word.len();
                    current_key = Some(word);
                    for _ in 0..wlen { chars.next(); }
                    chars.next();
                    continue;
                }
            }
            if current_key.is_some() {
                current_val.push(ch);
            }
        }
        if let Some(k) = current_key.take() {
            parts.push((k, current_val));
        }

        for (k, v) in parts {
            match k.as_str() {
                "pid" => w.pid = v.parse().ok(),
                "class" => w.class = unesc(&v),
                "instance" => w.instance = unesc(&v),
                "title" => w.title = unesc(&v),
                "exe" => w.exe_path = unesc(&v),
                "kind" => {
                    w.kind = match v.as_str() {
                        "native" => BinaryKind::Native,
                        "wine" => BinaryKind::Wine,
                        "flatpak" => BinaryKind::Flatpak,
                        _ => BinaryKind::Unknown,
                    }
                }
                "active" => w.is_active = v == "1",
                "hidden" => w.is_hidden = v == "1",
                "icon" => {
                    if v != "-" {
                        w.icon_path = Some(unesc(&v));
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        out.push(w);
    }
    out
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/tray.rs ===
```
//! Состояние трея: обёртка над `dbus::watcher::Watcher` + кеш иконок.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use crate::dbus::watcher::{self, IconPixmapData, TrayItem, Watcher};
use crate::dbus::DBus;
use crate::icons;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct TrayOut {
    pub dest: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub icon_path: Option<String>,
    pub needs_attention: bool,
    pub status: String,
}

pub struct TrayState {
    watcher: Watcher,
    icon_cache: HashMap<String, Option<String>>,
}

impl TrayState {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            watcher: Watcher::new(),
            icon_cache: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&mut self, dbus: &mut DBus) -> Result<(), String> {
        eprintln!("[dbus][tray] subscribing...");
        self.watcher.subscribe(dbus)?;
        eprintln!(
            "[dbus][tray] subscribed, {} items",
            self.watcher.items().len()
        );
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn is_loaded(&self) -> bool {
        self.watcher.is_loaded()
    }

    pub fn handle_message(
        &mut self,
        dbus: &mut DBus,
        msg: &rustbus::message_builder::MarshalledMessage,
    ) -> bool {
        match self.watcher.classify(msg) {
            None => false,
            Some(watcher::ReloadKind::List) => {
                eprintln!("[dbus][tray] list changed, reloading...");
                match self.watcher.reload_list(dbus) {
                    Ok(()) => {
                        eprintln!(
                            "[dbus][tray] reloaded, {} items",
                            self.watcher.items().len()
                        );
                        self.icon_cache.clear();
                        true
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("[dbus][tray] reload failed: {e}");
                        false
                    }
                }
            }
            Some(watcher::ReloadKind::Item(dest, path)) => {
                eprintln!(
                    "[dbus][tray] item changed dest='{}' path='{}'",
                    dest, path
                );
                if self.watcher.refresh_item(dbus, &dest, &path) {
                    if let Some(it) = self
                        .watcher
                        .items()
                        .iter()
                        .find(|i| i.dest == dest && i.path == path)
                    {
                        let key = pixmap_key(it);
                        self.icon_cache.remove(&it.icon_name);
                        self.icon_cache.remove(&key);
                    }
                    true
                } else {
                    false
                }
            }
            Some(watcher::ReloadKind::RemoveByDest(dest)) => {
                eprintln!("[dbus][tray] dest '{}' lost owner, removing item", dest);
                self.watcher.remove_by_dest(&dest)
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn snapshot(&mut self) -> Vec<TrayOut> {
        // Клонируем items, чтобы не держать immutable borrow на self.watcher,
        // пока resolve_icon мутирует self (icon_cache).
        let items: Vec<TrayItem> = self.watcher.items().to_vec();
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
        for it in &items {
            let icon_path = self.resolve_icon(it);
            out.push(TrayOut {
                dest: it.dest.clone(),
                path: it.path.clone(),
                title: it.title.clone(),
                icon_path,
                needs_attention: it.needs_attention,
                status: it.status.clone(),
            });
        }
        out
    }

    fn resolve_icon(&mut self, it: &TrayItem) -> Option<String> {
        // 1. IconName (файл).
        if !it.icon_name.is_empty() {
            if let Some(cached) = self.icon_cache.get(&it.icon_name) {
                return cached.clone();
            }
            let resolved =
                icons::resolve_icon_name(&it.icon_name).map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
            self.icon_cache
                .insert(it.icon_name.clone(), resolved.clone());
            if resolved.is_some() {
                return resolved;
            }
        }

        // 2. IconPixmap (raw ARGB) → сохранить в кеш, вернуть путь.
        if let Some(pix) = &it.icon_pixmap {
            let key = pixmap_key(it);
            if let Some(cached) = self.icon_cache.get(&key) {
                return cached.clone();
            }
            let resolved = save_pixmap_icon(&key, pix);
            self.icon_cache.insert(key, resolved.clone());
            return resolved;
        }

        None
    }

    pub fn activate(
        &self,
        dbus: &mut DBus,
        dest: &str,
        path: &str,
        button: u8,
        x: i32,
        y: i32,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.watcher.activate(dbus, dest, path, button, x, y)
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn items(&self) -> &[TrayItem] {
        self.watcher.items()
    }
}

/// Ключ кеша для IconPixmap. `title` стабильнее `dest` (dest меняется
/// при перезапуске приложения). Если `title` пустой — fallback на `path`.
fn pixmap_key(it: &TrayItem) -> String {
    if !it.title.is_empty() {
        format!("pixmap:{}", it.title)
    } else {
        format!("pixmap:{}", it.path)
    }
}

/// ARGB32 (A, R, G, B) → RGBA.
fn argb_to_rgba(argb: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(argb.len());
    for chunk in argb.chunks(4) {
        if chunk.len() < 4 {
            break;
        }
        let (a, r, g, b) = (chunk[0], chunk[1], chunk[2], chunk[3]);
        out.extend_from_slice(&[r, g, b, a]);
    }
    out
}

/// Сохранить IconPixmap в кеш nsbridge (нормализованный 64×64 PNG).
fn save_pixmap_icon(key: &str, pix: &IconPixmapData) -> Option<String> {
    if pix.width <= 0 || pix.height <= 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let rgba = argb_to_rgba(&pix.argb);
    let target = icons::ICON_CACHE_SIZE as usize;
    let normalized: Vec<u8> = if pix.width as usize == target && pix.height as usize == target {
        rgba
    } else {
        icons::resize_public(
            &rgba,
            pix.width as usize,
            pix.height as usize,
            target,
            target,
        )
    };
    let saved =
        icons::store::save_icon_rgba(key, icons::ICON_CACHE_SIZE, icons::ICON_CACHE_SIZE, &normalized);
    saved.map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/window.rs ===
```
//! Структуры данных окна. Сериализуются в JSON для отправки клиентам.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum BinaryKind {
    Native,
    Wine,
    Flatpak,
    Unknown,
}

impl BinaryKind {
    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            BinaryKind::Native => "native",
            BinaryKind::Wine => "wine",
            BinaryKind::Flatpak => "flatpak",
            BinaryKind::Unknown => "unknown",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WindowInfo {
    pub id: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub pid: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub title: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub class: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub instance: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub exe_path: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_kind")]
    pub kind: BinaryKind,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub is_active: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub is_hidden: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub icon_path: Option<String>,
}

impl WindowInfo {
    /// Стабильный ключ для группировки (наш «AppId»).
    pub fn app_key(&self) -> String {
        if !self.exe_path.is_empty() {
            self.exe_path.clone()
        } else if !self.class.is_empty() {
            format!("class:{}", self.class)
        } else {
            format!("id:{}", self.id)
        }
    }

    /// Человекочитаемый лейбл для UI.
    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
        if !self.class.is_empty() {
            self.class.clone()
        } else if !self.title.is_empty() {
            self.title.clone()
        } else {
            format!("0x{:x}", self.id)
        }
    }
}

fn default_kind() -> BinaryKind {
    BinaryKind::Unknown
}```

=== FILE: nsbridge/src/x11.rs ===
```
//! X11: подключение, атомы, чтение EWMH-свойств, сборка WindowInfo,
//! управление окнами (activate, toggle: minimize/unminimize).
//!
//! ВАЖНО:
//! - KWin не понимает `_NET_WM_STATE_ADD _NET_WM_STATE_HIDDEN` для сворачивания.
//!   Сворачиваем через `WM_CHANGE_STATE` (ICCCM, IconicState) — как `xdotool`.
//! - `_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW` может указывать на фрейм (не клиент).
//!   Фокус определяем по `_NET_WM_STATE_FOCUSED` (на клиенте).
//! - `subscribe_all_windows()` — подписка на `_NET_WM_STATE` всех окон
//!   (для отслеживания сворачивания/фокуса).
//!
//! Фильтр служебных окон (`is_normal_window`):
//! - KDE (plasmashell) выставляет панели сразу несколько типов:
//!   `DOCK`, `_KDE_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_OVERRIDE`, `NORMAL`. Наличие `NORMAL`
//!   не делает окно нормальным, если есть служебный тип.
//! - Поэтому: если есть хоть один служебный тип (`DOCK`, `DESKTOP`,
//!   `TOOLBAR`, `MENU`, `SPLASH`, `UTILITY`, `DIALOG`, `POPUP_MENU`,
//!   `TOOLTIP`, `NOTIFICATION`, `COMBO`, `DROPDOWN_MENU`, KDE override) —
//!   окно НЕ нормальное. `SKIP_TASKBAR`/`SKIP_PAGER` — тоже.
//! - Только если служебных нет и есть `NORMAL` (или типов нет вообще,
//!   EWMH default = NORMAL) — окно нормальное.

use x11rb::connection::Connection;
use x11rb::protocol::xproto::{
    AtomEnum, ChangeWindowAttributesAux, ClientMessageData, ClientMessageEvent,
    ConnectionExt as _, EventMask, Window,
};
use x11rb::rust_connection::RustConnection;

use crate::icons;
use crate::proc;
use crate::window::{BinaryKind, WindowInfo};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Atoms {
    pub net_client_list: u32,
    pub net_active_window: u32,
    pub net_wm_pid: u32,
    pub net_wm_name: u32,
    pub wm_name: u32,
    pub wm_class: u32,
    pub net_wm_state: u32,
    pub net_wm_state_hidden: u32,
    pub net_wm_state_focused: u32,
    pub net_wm_state_skip_taskbar: u32,
    pub net_wm_state_skip_pager: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_normal: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_dock: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_desktop: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_toolbar: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_menu: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_splash: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_utility: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_dialog: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_popup_menu: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_tooltip: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_notification: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_combo: u32,
    pub net_wm_window_type_dropdown_menu: u32,
    pub kde_net_wm_window_type_override: u32,
    pub wm_change_state: u32,
}

pub struct X11 {
    pub conn: RustConnection,
    pub screen_num: usize,
    pub root: Window,
    pub atoms: Atoms,
}

impl X11 {
    pub fn connect() -> Result<Self, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let (conn, screen_num) = x11rb::connect(None)?;
        let root = conn.setup().roots[screen_num].root;

        conn.change_window_attributes(
            root,
            &ChangeWindowAttributesAux::new().event_mask(EventMask::PROPERTY_CHANGE),
        )?;
        conn.flush()?;

        let intern = |name: &[u8]| -> Result<u32, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
            Ok(conn.intern_atom(false, name)?.reply()?.atom)
        };

        let atoms = Atoms {
            net_client_list: intern(b"_NET_CLIENT_LIST")?,
            net_active_window: intern(b"_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW")?,
            net_wm_pid: intern(b"_NET_WM_PID")?,
            net_wm_name: intern(b"_NET_WM_NAME")?,
            wm_name: intern(b"WM_NAME")?,
            wm_class: intern(b"WM_CLASS")?,
            net_wm_state: intern(b"_NET_WM_STATE")?,
            net_wm_state_hidden: intern(b"_NET_WM_STATE_HIDDEN")?,
            net_wm_state_focused: intern(b"_NET_WM_STATE_FOCUSED")?,
            net_wm_state_skip_taskbar: intern(b"_NET_WM_STATE_SKIP_TASKBAR")?,
            net_wm_state_skip_pager: intern(b"_NET_WM_STATE_SKIP_PAGER")?,
            net_wm_window_type: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE")?,
            net_wm_window_type_normal: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_NORMAL")?,
            net_wm_window_type_dock: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DOCK")?,
            net_wm_window_type_desktop: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DESKTOP")?,
            net_wm_window_type_toolbar: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_TOOLBAR")?,
            net_wm_window_type_menu: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_MENU")?,
            net_wm_window_type_splash: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_SPLASH")?,
            net_wm_window_type_utility: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_UTILITY")?,
            net_wm_window_type_dialog: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DIALOG")?,
            net_wm_window_type_popup_menu: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_POPUP_MENU")?,
            net_wm_window_type_tooltip: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_TOOLTIP")?,
            net_wm_window_type_notification: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_NOTIFICATION")?,
            net_wm_window_type_combo: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_COMBO")?,
            net_wm_window_type_dropdown_menu: intern(b"_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_DROPDOWN_MENU")?,
            kde_net_wm_window_type_override: intern(b"_KDE_NET_WM_WINDOW_TYPE_OVERRIDE")?,
            wm_change_state: intern(b"WM_CHANGE_STATE")?,
        };

        Ok(Self { conn, screen_num, root, atoms })
    }

    /// Подписаться на PropertyChange всех окон из _NET_CLIENT_LIST.
    /// Нужно, чтобы ловить _NET_WM_STATE (сворачивание/фокус) каждого окна.
    /// Идемпотентно (повторный вызов — обновляет маску, не ломает).
    pub fn subscribe_all_windows(&self) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let client_list = match self.conn
            .get_property(false, self.root, self.atoms.net_client_list, AtomEnum::WINDOW, 0, 1024)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|c| c.reply().ok())
        {
            Some(r) => r.value32().map(|it| it.collect::<Vec<Window>>()).unwrap_or_default(),
            None => Vec::new(),
        };
        for win in client_list {
            let _ = self.conn.change_window_attributes(
                win,
                &ChangeWindowAttributesAux::new().event_mask(EventMask::PROPERTY_CHANGE),
            );
        }
        let _ = self.conn.flush();
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn prop_u32(&self, win: Window, atom: u32) -> Option<u32> {
        let r = self.conn
            .get_property(false, win, atom, AtomEnum::CARDINAL, 0, 1)
            .ok()?
            .reply()
            .ok()?;
        if r.value_len == 0 || r.value.len() < 4 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(u32::from_ne_bytes([r.value[0], r.value[1], r.value[2], r.value[3]]))
    }

    pub fn prop_string(&self, win: Window, atom: u32) -> Option<String> {
        let r = self.conn
            .get_property(false, win, atom, AtomEnum::ANY, 0, 1024)
            .ok()?
            .reply()
            .ok()?;
        if r.value_len == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.value).to_string();
        let s = s.trim_matches('\0').trim().to_string();
        if s.is_empty() { None } else { Some(s) }
    }

    pub fn prop_wm_class(&self, win: Window) -> (String, String) {
        let r = self.conn
            .get_property(false, win, self.atoms.wm_class, AtomEnum::STRING, 0, 64)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|c| c.reply().ok());
        if let Some(r) = r {
            if r.value_len > 0 {
                let parts: Vec<String> = r.value
                    .split(|&b| b == 0)
                    .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                    .map(|s| String::from_utf8_lossy(s).to_string())
                    .collect();
                let instance = parts.get(0).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
                let class = parts.get(1).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
                return (instance, class);
            }
        }
        (String::new(), String::new())
    }

    pub fn prop_atoms(&self, win: Window, atom: u32) -> Vec<u32> {
        let r = self.conn
            .get_property(false, win, atom, AtomEnum::ATOM, 0, 32)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|c| c.reply().ok());
        if let Some(r) = r {
            r.value32().map(|it| it.collect()).unwrap_or_default()
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        }
    }

    /// Служебные типы окон (не показывать в таскбаре, не активировать).
    fn service_types(&self) -> [u32; 14] {
        [
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_dock,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_desktop,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_toolbar,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_menu,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_splash,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_utility,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_dialog,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_popup_menu,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_tooltip,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_notification,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_combo,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_dropdown_menu,
            self.atoms.kde_net_wm_window_type_override,
            self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_normal, // проверяется отдельно, не в цикле
        ]
    }

    fn is_normal_window(&self, win: Window) -> bool {
        let states = self.prop_atoms(win, self.atoms.net_wm_state);
        if states.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_state_skip_taskbar)
            || states.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_state_skip_pager)
        {
            return false;
        }

        let types = self.prop_atoms(win, self.atoms.net_wm_window_type);
        if types.is_empty() {
            // EWMH default: нет типа = NORMAL.
            return true;
        }

        // Если есть хоть один служебный тип — это не обычное окно,
        // даже если вместе с ним выставлен NORMAL (так делает KDE
        // для панелей: DOCK + OVERRIDE + NORMAL).
        let service = self.service_types();
        for t in &service[..13] {
            if types.contains(t) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        // Служебных нет. Обычное — только если есть NORMAL
        // (или типов нет, но это уже обработано выше).
        types.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_window_type_normal)
    }

    pub fn collect_windows(&self) -> Vec<WindowInfo> {
        let client_list = match self.conn
            .get_property(false, self.root, self.atoms.net_client_list, AtomEnum::WINDOW, 0, 1024)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|c| c.reply().ok())
        {
            Some(r) => r.value32().map(|it| it.collect::<Vec<Window>>()).unwrap_or_default(),
            None => Vec::new(),
        };

        let mut windows = Vec::new();
        for win in client_list {
            if !self.is_normal_window(win) {
                continue;
            }

            let pid = self.prop_u32(win, self.atoms.net_wm_pid);
            let title = self.prop_string(win, self.atoms.net_wm_name)
                .or_else(|| self.prop_string(win, self.atoms.wm_name))
                .unwrap_or_default();
            let (instance, class) = self.prop_wm_class(win);

            if title.is_empty() && class.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            let (exe_path, kind) = match pid {
                Some(p) => proc::resolve_exe(p),
                None => (String::new(), BinaryKind::Unknown),
            };

            let states = self.prop_atoms(win, self.atoms.net_wm_state);
            let is_hidden = states.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_state_hidden);
            let is_active = states.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_state_focused);

            let icon_path = icons::resolve_icon_path(&exe_path, &class)
                .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());

            windows.push(WindowInfo {
                id: win,
                pid,
                title,
                class,
                instance,
                exe_path,
                kind,
                is_active,
                is_hidden,
                icon_path,
            });
        }
        windows
    }

    /// Сфокусировать окно (`_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW`).
    pub fn activate_window(&self, win: Window) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let ev = ClientMessageEvent::new(
            32,
            win,
            self.atoms.net_active_window,
            ClientMessageData::from([1u32, x11rb::CURRENT_TIME, 0, 0, 0]),
        );
        self.conn.send_event(
            false,
            self.root,
            EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_REDIRECT | EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_NOTIFY,
            ev,
        )?;
        self.conn.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Свернуть окно (`WM_CHANGE_STATE` → `IconicState`).
    /// ICCCM. Как `xdotool windowminimize`.
    pub fn minimize_window(&self, win: Window) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let ev = ClientMessageEvent::new(
            32,
            win,
            self.atoms.wm_change_state,
            ClientMessageData::from([3u32, 0, 0, 0, 0]), // 3 = IconicState
        );
        self.conn.send_event(
            false,
            self.root,
            EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_REDIRECT | EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_NOTIFY,
            ev,
        )?;
        self.conn.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Развернуть окно: снять `_NET_WM_STATE_HIDDEN` (REMOVE = 0) + фокус.
    pub fn unminimize_window(&self, win: Window) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let ev = ClientMessageEvent::new(
            32,
            win,
            self.atoms.net_wm_state,
            ClientMessageData::from([0u32, self.atoms.net_wm_state_hidden, 0u32, 1u32, 0u32]), // REMOVE = 0
        );
        self.conn.send_event(
            false,
            self.root,
            EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_REDIRECT | EventMask::SUBSTRUCTURE_NOTIFY,
            ev,
        )?;
        self.conn.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Toggle окна:
    /// фокус (`_NET_WM_STATE_FOCUSED`) → свернуть (`WM_CHANGE_STATE`);
    /// не фокус → активировать (развернуть + фокус).
    pub fn toggle_window(&self, win: Window) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        // Защита: служебные окна (DOCK, DESKTOP, ...) не активируем и не
        // сворачиваем. Если они как-то попали в groups (баг фильтра),
        // toggle для них — no-op, чтобы не дёргать панель KDE.
        if !self.is_normal_window(win) {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let states = self.prop_atoms(win, self.atoms.net_wm_state);
        let is_active = states.contains(&self.atoms.net_wm_state_focused);

        if is_active {
            self.minimize_window(win)?;
        } else {
            self.activate_window(win)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}```
